# Rust exit: verified behavioral parity

Generated by `node Tools/agent/rust-parity-fingerprint.mjs`; do not edit. Rows: `verified.tsv`.

Rust tree `020d8527e50255391fcc3613fdc5736fb9c26732`; Jet tree `a2f3a6867ac6762e3a7ee3cb047c0f6a20ed3826`; secondary Jet trees `origin/port/main` (`61693bcbd7cc`), `cutover-ready` (`98a9c40469b2`).

Scope: every Rust item of the compiler (jet-lexer, jet-parser, jet-sema, jet-comptime, jet-evalhost, jet-codegen, jet-driver, jet-foundation, jet-cli, `Source/`), the Core library (`crates/jet-codegen/src/Prelude/`) and the runtime (jet-rt), test files excluded, plus every live Core runtime route whose C export no Jet runtime `#Export(c)` defines.

Method: each Rust item's fingerprint (diagnostic codes, message strings, enum variants constructed, numeric literals, callee names, branch count; fields, variants and methods for types) is scored against candidate Jet items (the Core call registry's body for a Prelude symbol, the name ledger's pointer, same-name and Prelude-spelling items, and a fingerprint search over the whole Jet tree), each taken together with the Jet items it calls in its own package. Score = matched weight / Rust weight (code 5, message 3, variant 1.5, number 1, member 1, callee 0.5, single word 0.5); a fingerprint-only hit outside the area's directories whose name shares no word with the Rust name loses 0.15.

- `PORTED`: score >= 0.70 and every diagnostic code present; or, for a thin item (fingerprint weight < 2), the registry's body or a same-name item in the area's Jet directories (`basis` `thin:*`).
- `PARTIAL`: the best Jet counterpart scores 0.30-0.70, misses a diagnostic code, or is the registry's body but scores lower; `missing` lists the absent codes, messages, variants, members, numbers and a branch collapse.
- `PORTED-UNMERGED`: the best counterpart exists only on a secondary tree, in a file that tree compiled.
- `DRAFT-ONLY`: the best counterpart exists only on a secondary tree, in a Compiler file outside that tree's compiler unit (the member files of its compiler packages; `Compiler/Bootstrap/sources.list` on a tree from before the package form) or a Jetpack CLI file: an uncompiled transliteration, not a port.
- `ABSENT`: no Jet counterpart scores 0.30 (`missing` names the nearest miss); `basis` `route-census` marks a live Core route whose export no Jet runtime `#Export(c)` defines.
- `OBSOLETE`: retired with Rust; `reason` is the ledger drop reason, the reviewed triage group rationale, or Rust std-trait glue.

## Porting work packages

Every ABSENT and PARTIAL row belongs to one package (`package` column of `verified.tsv`). A package is about 600 Rust lines of porting (a PARTIAL row costs a third of its lines), about 2-4 hours. Targets are the Jet files the same Rust files' ported rows already live in.
